# Agents · D · part 17

896 entries, design-referee to design-system-architect. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **design-referee**: Read-only evidence referee. Use after independent Design Tribunal critics to reconcile findings, preserve disagreement, reject unsupported claims, derive release status, and produce the minimum repair queue. ([Quirk-Systems/project-scaffold](https://github.com/Quirk-Systems/project-scaffold/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-referee.md) · ★1)
- **design-reference-research**: Spawned by design-review (reference-research step); not for direct use. ([davidgarciagordo/design-review](https://github.com/davidgarciagordo/design-review/blob/HEAD/agents/design-reference-research.md))
- **design-refiner**: Review and refine an existing interface with the purchased Emil skills. Use for a UI polish pass or design refinement. ([stephendolan/dotfiles](https://github.com/stephendolan/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/ai/agents/design-refiner.md) · ★2)

It should NOT be invoked directly by users. ([isdlc/isdlc-knowledge-service](https://github.com/isdlc/isdlc-knowledge-service/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/03-design-refiner.md))

Spawned by /gdd:audit (end-of-cycle) and /gdd:reflect (on-demand). ([hegemonart/get-design-done](https://github.com/hegemonart/get-design-done/blob/HEAD/agents/design-reflector.md) · ★3)

- **design-research**: Searches and inspects legitimate design references, a live site, a template, a design system, and extracts the layout, typography, interaction and animation patterns worth reusing, keeping reference, inspiration, pattern and implementation strictly apart. - **design-reviewer**: Reviews a study design before any production run. Reports only; never edits the records and never chooses for the researcher. Use when a design is about to be frozen, before the first production run. ([akontadakis/paper-skills](https://github.com/akontadakis/paper-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: Adversarial post-implementation visual reviewer. Invoke after a feature slice is implemented and engineering tests pass, before merging. Runs Playwright against the live docker stack to compare the real feature route against its canonical /preview/&lt;feature&gt;. Reports visual + behavioral deltas. Parallel to code-reviewer (which attacks the engineering diff). ([alfredoctm/whatthefunnel](https://github.com/alfredoctm/whatthefunnel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: Reviews UI code against the project's design language and principles. Use after any change to packages/ui, apps/web, or apps/admin. Read-only. ([Ali-Traboulsi/ali-traboulsi-portfolio](https://github.com/Ali-Traboulsi/ali-traboulsi-portfolio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: Independent UI/visual design reviewer for Sababi. Judges real screenshots against the design decisions and living-room usability, with no stake in the earlier design choices. Use for visual design reviews. ([alonzo86/remote-play](https://github.com/alonzo86/remote-play/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: Independent visual QA for a page or feature. Use after building any full page or multi-component feature. ([AndrewDongminYoo/focus-study-landing](https://github.com/AndrewDongminYoo/focus-study-landing/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)

- **design-reviewer**: Product Design Harness teammate that reviews the rendered page blind, knowing only the brief and the spec. Opens every state in the preview, with data of every size, judges the page as a whole and measures: success criteria and principles, state coverage, the project's rules and decisions, the Figma frame when there is one, copy against the glossary, values the design system doesn't have, accessibility checked by hand, the markers of generated-looking UI, prototype honesty and deceptive patterns. Returns a verdict (pass, fix or decide) with findings that each carry a measurement and a source.… ([arez-xd/product-design-harness](https://github.com/arez-xd/product-design-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/design-reviewer.md))

- **design-reviewer**: Read-only design review in two modes. Draft spec mode: checks a spec folder's design/screens.md against the design-system skill before freeze. Build mode: fifth pipeline reviewer — starts the app on the test DB, screenshots every changed screen (light + dark, desktop + tablet), runs axe, compares with the skill and the spec's screens. - **design-sync**: Use when the user wants to MIRROR an existing Figma file into code with 1:1 fidelity and no hallucination — NOT generate a new design. Reads a Figma frame, looks each node up in a component bridge, and emits only what's mapped; anything unmapped becomes an explicit GAP marker, never an invented component. Also runs a divergence report between an existing Figma file and existing code (`--mode diff`). Distinct from `design-engineer`/`figma-designer`, which GENERATE from a lo-fi handoff. Requires `mcp__figma`; uses `mcp__playwright` for screenshot verification when available. ([KaungMyatHein/agent-harry](https://github.com/KaungMyatHein/agent-harry/blob/HEAD/templates/.claude/agents/design-sync.md))
- **design-sync**: Use for cross-tier or shared-contract work to verify backend docs, frontend docs, SPECs, ADRs, and the OpenAPI contract agree on behavior, data shape, errors, auth, migrations, and acceptance criteria.
